Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in microbiology theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 685 and the invariant I(A,B) = 798 mod 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to microbiology theory, we decompose the problem into 685 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 798. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
